# atx-vol ledger — append-only durable facts

Protocol: one line per fact, newest last. Format: `YYYY-MM-DD | area | fact | source`. Never edit or delete existing lines — a correction is a NEW line citing the line it supersedes. Grep this file before re-deriving build traps, measured numbers, or decisions. Append only facts worth remembering across sessions: gate results, discovered traps, measured baselines, decisions with rationale. Keep entries one line; long rationale belongs in `sprints/` or `docs/` with a pointer here.

2026-08-16 CORRECTION: the b5142b2a merge message called the anchored-eSSVI fit-quality figure (0.3557 -> 0.3208 half-spreads) a "mean absolute pricing error". That statistic does not exist in the tree. The real metric is BandAuditRow::avg_signed_err_half_spreads (surface_db_admin.cpp:641-671, emitted as avg_signed_hs by `atx-vol-surface-db band-audit`), a per-expiry MEAN SIGNED error in which signed errors cancel within an expiry before |.| is applied. The anchored lane's own report labelled it correctly as "quote-weighted mean |avg signed error|"; the mislabel was introduced in the merge message. Verified values reproduce at 0.3553 -> 0.3205.
2026-08-16 The anchored-eSSVI "fits better" conclusion is corroborated by three independent statistics on 6,512 matched (symbol, expiry) pairs / 408,554 quotes: in-band fraction 0.9113 -> 0.9196 (a genuine per-quote absolute criterion), mean|signed| 0.3553 -> 0.3205, and mean per-expiry max_abs 4.6995 -> 4.1598. cells_ok 546 -> 560.
2026-08-16 TRAP: in-band fit-quality numbers are NOT comparable across sources without checking three axes. band-audit in-band (0.9113 SP100-subset 0.889) covers the FULL chain; session.cpp:2466 mean_frac_within_bidask (0.9146) covers ADMITTED ROWS ONLY and is an unweighted board-mean; and universe composition alone moves it (SP100 0.8909 vs xsec 0.9111). Two lanes reported 0.9112 and 0.9146 as if they were the same baseline; they never were.

Auditing 3,117 symbols on ONE session decoded ~159 GB. 2026-08-23 | storage | PERSISTING CarrySource IS ADDITIVE AND FORCES NO REBUILD, BUT THE OBVIOUS CHEAP VERSION OF IT IS A SILENT-CORRUPTION HAZARD IN BOTH DIRECTIONS. ArchiveV2SurfaceHeader is pinned at 256 bytes with ten u64 column offsets and a reserved[66] tail (surface_archive.hpp:484-520), so an eleventh column offset carves 8 bytes out of reserved, keeps sizeof at 256, keeps the sizeof-fold in schema_hash_v2 identical, and leaves every existing prodv1 / prodv1-carry partition openable - 1 byte per slice, no rebuild. TWO TRAPS. (1) v2_column_ok(off=0, elem=1, count=n, rs, align=1) RETURNS TRUE - 0%1==0, 0<=rs, n<=rs-0 - so a new reader on an OLD archive, where the reserved bytes are zero, would validate offset 0 as a legal column and read the record header's own magic bytes back as CarrySource values. The reader MUST treat offset 0 as ABSENT before it calls the validator. (2) the salt must be bumped ANYWAY even though the layout is byte-identical, so a pre-change reader REJECTS a new archive that actually sets the column instead of silently ignoring provenance it cannot see; the prior-salt accept-list keeps every old archive openable. 2026-08-23 | fit | HYPOTHESIS (FROM CODE READING, NOT YET MEASURED) FOR WHY THE 5-PAIR CAP DOES NOT EQUALIZE THIN AND DENSE CHAINS. max_borrow_pairs=5 caps retained pairs regardless of availability, so a 31-pair name and a 9-pair name both fit on at most 5 - the cap alone should have made them behave alike, and measurably it does not. select_carry_pairs (deamer.cpp:561-578) sets k = min(min(max(n_atm, band_count), max_borrow_pairs), n_valid) where band_count counts pairs inside carry_atm_band=0.06, then partial_sorts by |K-S|. On a DENSE grid band_count >= 5, so the five selected all sit inside +/-6% moneyness and are essentially the same strikes from one session to the next. On a SPARSE grid band_count collapses toward 0, k falls back to max(n_atm=3, band_count)=3, and those three nearest-by-|K-S| pairs can sit well OUTSIDE the band - deeper strikes, wider relative spreads, more American early-exercise premium in the parity residual - and the SELECTED SET ITSELF can change composition overnight as spot drifts across a wide strike grid. TWO PREDICTIONS THAT WOULD CONFIRM OR KILL IT: the selected pairs' moneyness should be systematically wider on churning names, and the day-over-day change in the selected strike SET should be larger for them. The trace does not currently record selected-pair moneyness, so testing this needs one more field | deamer.cpp:561-578, unmeasured

2026-10-16 | markov-seed | SIGMA-INTERVAL ONE-SIDED SEEDS ARE REFUSED (review MINOR-1 fixed). A one-sided interval (ask-only/bidless, or bid-only) used to close its open side with min_sigma/max_sigma, so the seed sqrt(bound*layout constant) became the initial value AND, under prior=seed, the prior centre that a flat hinge fit returns to exactly. It is now parameter status OneSidedInterval: no point seed, the market bound is reported (interval_lower/upper), and the tool keeps the configured initial and prior (state fallback_one_sided_interval, counted in initialization_states). For PerVarianceInterval, either differenced endpoint being one-sided refuses. Every m5/m6 prior=seed number predates this fix (18 of 53 m5 interval seeds were one-sided), so rerun before any held-out use. 2026-10-16 | markov-fit | LM JACOBIAN FAILURES ARE TYPED (review MINOR-2 and NIT-2 fixed). A refused probe is retried once in the other direction, within the evaluation budget. If a column still has no admitted probe, the search stops with termination JacobianFailure ("jacobian_failure"). Before, the column stayed zero and the gradient test ran on the partial gradient, so a fit with every probe refused reported gradient_tolerance. The primary probe now goes backward whenever the forward room to max_sigma is shorter than jacobian_log_step; before, it was truncated to as little as one ulp. Tests reach DampingLimit through a trial refused by the explicit-Euler work cap with max_damping == initial_damping. The Cholesky refusal is unreachable for a finite J'J, so it is tested on the solver directly | atx-vol/tests/american_markov_fit_test.cpp AmericanMarkovFitSearch.*
